Root cause of B (RED test timing defect, not an implementation defect)
- Q01 (L127), Q02a (L138) and Q03 (L160) read the pending count with a synchronous getByText immediately after the
  "Borrowing limit" region appeared. The region correctly appears first in its loading state (AC11-S4); the count arrives
  from the mocked API a moment later. The suite's other quota-value checks already awaited findByText.
- Correction e27cc10 changes only those three queries to awaited findByText (3 insertions, 3 deletions).
- STATEMENT: the timing correction did not change any acceptance criterion, matcher, expected assertion, expected
  outcome or fixture. It only waits (up to the default 1 s) for the same text.
- D shows the corrected suite is still a valid RED: on the pre-change source it fails the same 17 tests at the same lines
  (Q01/Q02a/Q03 still fail first at the missing panel, L126/L137/L159, before the corrected lines), and the same 4
  preserved-behavior tests pass. D is a post-correction baseline reproduction, not the original RED execution.

Pre-existing flaky tests (F and G) — not caused by #82
- Failing tests: navigation.test.tsx "redirects /requests to sign in…" (F) and borrower-navigation.test.tsx "signed out
  at /dashboard: redirected to sign in…" (G). Both assert window.location.pathname synchronously after the Sign In button
  appears, while the redirect runs in a later effect.
- An interleaved 5+5 measurement (matrix below) shows the same flake on the unchanged pre-change application (isolated
  checkout at e27cc10) and on 725307b: 1/5 failing runs each. #82 does not modify these tests; a separate fix is recommended.

Mocked UI verification (not production or full-stack evidence)
- artifacts/lab-07/cr-01/screenshots/frontend-green/ (README.md, 22 PNG, mocked-ui-verification.json, ui-verify.mocked.cjs)
- 9 scenarios × desktop 1280×800 and mobile 390×844: 18/18 PASS; horizontal overflow 0 px; malformed body → error state.

Post-review RED/GREEN verification — PR #89
=================================================================
Appended 2026-11-27 ICT by yiiipunn. Everything above this heading is the original evidence and is unchanged, including
its historical counts (21 tests, 101 regression). Prepared with AI assistance (Anthropic Claude ran the commands and
collected the logs under my direction). All results below come from the runs recorded in the linked raw logs.

Why this section exists
- DEV4952's independent review of 9bc49cb (https://github.com/gearstack-studio/kitloop/pull/89#issuecomment-7220149180)
  confirmed that the functional findings are resolved. It requested evidence for the final suite: 23 quota tests and
  103 regression tests instead of 21 and 101.
- Earlier review of e14b914 (https://github.com/gearstack-studio/kitloop/pull/89#issuecomment-7211888197): F-01 delayed
  409, F-02 flaky redirect assertions, F-03 catalog quota-error label and retry.

|---|---|---|---|
| Original RED (test-first) | d76751c (tests only, before any implementation) | frontend-red.txt | 17 failed, 4 passed (21) |

| Retrospective RED (this section) | 7a3db98 + final suite | not test-first; see note | 19 failed, 4 passed, 0 skipped (23) |

RETROSPECTIVE, not original. The RED below was run after the implementation existed. It uses the final suite against the
unchanged pre-change application, to show that the final suite (including the two tests added in 9bc49cb) fails on the
baseline. The original test-first RED is frontend-red.txt (d76751c, 17 failed / 4 passed of 21). It remains the
test-first record.

  The files actually run were byte-identical to the committed blob (LF, 0 CR bytes, same SHA-256). All three checkouts
  were created with core.autocrlf=false.
  For comparison only: a Windows CRLF working copy (core.autocrlf=true) of the same file hashes to
  fb228e736ff07df5520e258576a7a74e040f69cf75631fb02b9c9d6b876eb2c6. No run used CRLF bytes.
- Between 7a3db98 and 9bc49cb, client/ changes only client/src and three test files. Package, lock, Vite, Vitest,
  TypeScript and ESLint configuration are identical, so overlaying the single test file is a faithful RED.

Environment: Windows 11, Node v24.19.0, npm 11.3.0, Vitest 5.1.0 (client), jsdom tests with mocked HTTP. Each
checkout had `npm ci` run in client/. No backend, database or production system was used.

RED detail: the 4 passing tests are the preserved-behaviour cases Q04b, Q10, Q15 (flag false) and Q15 (flag absent). The
19 failures include all 17 original failures and the 2 tests added in 9bc49cb. On the baseline those 2 fail because no
quota UI exists ("expected [] to have a length of 2", "Unable to find role=button name /Borrowing limit unavailable:
Camera/").

Resolution of the review findings
- F-01 delayed HTTP 409 (AC11-S6 / TC11). The test "keeps submission disabled while the quota refresh after a 409 is
  pending" holds the quota re-read open after a 409 and asserts that exactly one POST /api/requests is sent.
  - Pre-fix e14b914: FAILS with "expected [ { method: 'POST', …(4) }, …(1) ] to have a length of 1 but got 2". This is
    DEV4952's two-POST reproduction.
  - 9bc49cb: PASSES. The fix sets the quota to loading before the re-read after a 409 or a successful submission, so the
    stale canSubmitRequest is not used.
  - Mocked browser check: submission is disabled while the re-read is held, and the POST count stays at 1 after a forced
    second click.
- F-03 catalog quota error. The test "shows a quota read failure on the catalog and lets the borrower retry there" checks
  that a failed quota read shows "Borrowing limit unavailable" (not "Checking limit"), offers "Retry borrowing limit",
  and recovers after Retry.
  - Pre-fix e14b914: FAILS ("Unable to find role=button name /Borrowing limit unavailable: Camera/").
  - 9bc49cb: PASSES.
  - Mocked browser check: the Retry button is focusable and works with the keyboard, and the catalog recovers after Retry.
- F-02 redirect assertions. In 9bc49cb, navigation.test.tsx and borrower-navigation.test.tsx await the pathname with
  waitFor after the Sign In button appears. All 3 full runs above passed 103/103. The same change is in merged PR #88
  (commit 970d6dc, identical patch).

Scope and limits
- This is mocked frontend verification (jsdom with mocked fetch, and Playwright with route interception). It is not
  full-stack or production verification.
- Backend PR #88 is merged into sprint-1-staging (e92512a, 2026-11-27 20:51 ICT). This branch has not been integrated or
  tested against it yet. Live-API verification of rejection at capacity and recovery after cancellation is still open.
